        <main property="mainContentOfPage" resource="#wb-main" typeof="WebPageElement" class="container">
            
            <div class="mwstitle section">

    <h1 property="name" id="wb-cont" dir="ltr">Designated learning institutions list</h1>
	</div>
<div class="mwsgeneric-base-html parbase section">



    <p>Search through a list of designated learning institutions (DLIs) to find the right school and program, and start planning for your studies in Canada.</p>
<p>You need a letter of acceptance (LOA) from a DLI to apply for a study permit. If you plan to attend a post-secondary school in Canada, make sure it’s on the list.</p>
<details>
  <summary>What’s a DLI?</summary>
  <p>A DLI is a school approved by a provincial or territorial government to host international students.</p>
  <p>All primary and secondary schools in Canada are DLIs. They aren’t included in this list.</p>
  <p>DLIs at the post-secondary level must verify letters of acceptance and report on the enrollment status of their international students. DLIs that don’t comply with these obligations may face consequences, including suspension from accepting international students. </p>
</details>
<details>
  <summary>How to find the right DLI</summary>
  <ol class="lst-spcd">
    <li>
    <p><strong>Select the type of DLI you’re looking for</strong></p>
    <p>Select a tab to display a list of DLIs based on the type of school you want to attend. Some&nbsp;schools offer</p>
    <ul>
      <li>programs eligible for a <a href="/en/immigration-refugees-citizenship/services/study-canada/work/after-graduation/about.html">post-graduation work permit (PGWP)</a></li>
    <li>graduate degree-granting programs that <strong>don’t </strong>require <a href="/en/immigration-refugees-citizenship/services/study-canada/study-permit/get-documents/provincial-attestation-letter.html">a provincial attestation letter (PAL) or a territorial attestation letter (TAL)</a></li>
    </ul>
    </li>
    <li>
    <p><strong>Choose a province or territory</strong></p>
    <p>Explore schools based on where you want to study.</p>
  </li>
    <li>
    <p><strong>Search and browse</strong></p>
    <p>You can search the list by typing in keywords such as the name of the school, the city, etc.</p>
  </li>
    <li>
    <p><strong>Find the DLI number</strong></p>
    <p>Find the DLI number for the school you want to attend and write it under the section <strong>Details of intended study in Canada</strong> on your study permit application.</p>
  </li>
  </ol>
</details>


</div>
<div class="mwsgeneric-base-html parbase section">

        <details id="pgwp-eligible">
            <summary><span class="hidden-xs hidden-sm"><span class="fas fa-briefcase fa-3x mrgn-tp-md" aria-hidden="true"></span><br></span><span class="h4 mrgn-tp-0">Offers PGWP-eligible programs</span></summary>
            <div class="d-flex flex-column highlight highlight-secondary">
                <div class="highlight-padding">
                    <h2 class="mrgn-tp-sm">DLIs offering PGWP-eligible programs</h2>
                    <p>If you’re looking to work in Canada after graduating from a DLI, this list includes all DLIs that currently offer programs of study that make you eligible for the PGWP.</p>
                    <p>Not all programs offered by a DLI are eligible for a PGWP. Make sure to check the details for a list of eligible programs offered by the DLI.</p>
                    <details>
                        <summary>All PGWP eligibility requirements</summary>
                        <div class="row-no-gutters brdr-0">
                            <p>If your DLI offers PGWP-eligible programs, you must also <strong>meet all</strong> the <a href="/en/immigration-refugees-citizenship/services/study-canada/work/after-graduation/eligibility.html">PGWP eligibility requirements</a>.</p>
<p>PGWP eligibility requirements include</p>
                            <ul>

<li><a href="/en/immigration-refugees-citizenship/services/study-canada/work/after-graduation/eligibility.html#general">general eligibility</a></li>
<li>eligibility requirements based on <a href="/en/immigration-refugees-citizenship/services/study-canada/work/after-graduation/eligibility.html#education-level">study program</a>, including
<ul>
<li><a href="/en/immigration-refugees-citizenship/services/study-canada/work/after-graduation/eligibility/language-results.html">proof of language skills</a>, and</li>
<li><a href="/en/immigration-refugees-citizenship/services/study-canada/work/after-graduation/eligibility/field-of-study.html">field of study requirements</a> (where applicable)</li>
</ul>
</li>

                            </ul>
                        </div>
                    </details>

        <details id="public">
            <summary><span class="hidden-xs hidden-sm"><span class="fas fa-graduation-cap fa-3x mrgn-tp-md" aria-hidden="true"></span><br></span><span class="h4 mrgn-tp-0">Graduate degree programs at public DLIs</span></summary>
            <div class="d-flex flex-column highlight highlight-last">
                <div class="highlight-padding">
                    <h2 class="mrgn-tp-sm">Public DLIs offering PAL/TAL-exempt graduate programs</h2>
                    <p>If you’re looking for a degree-granting graduate program, this list includes all <strong>public</strong> DLIs that currently offer <strong>degree-granting graduate programs </strong>at the master’s and doctorate levels.</p>
                    <p>You <strong>don’t </strong>need a PAL or TAL when you apply for a study permit to attend one of these programs if you’re applying after January&nbsp;1, 2026.</p>
                    <p>You still need an LOA from the school and <a href="/en/immigration-refugees-citizenship/services/study-canada/study-permit/get-documents.html">other documents</a> when you apply for a study permit.</p>
                    <p>If you’re planning to study in Quebec, make sure to <a href="/en/immigration-refugees-citizenship/services/study-canada/study-permit/get-documents/provincial-attestation-letter.html#a2">check the province’s requirements</a>.</p>
                    <div class="wb-frmvld mrgn-bttm-md nojs-hide">
                        <form>

                                <ul>
                                    <li data-wb-fieldflow="[
                        {&quot;action&quot;: &quot;tblfilter&quot;,&quot;source&quot;: &quot;#dli-table-grad&quot;,&quot;column&quot;: 0,&quot;value&quot;: &quot;Alberta&quot;},
                        {&quot;action&quot;:&quot;ajax&quot;, &quot;type&quot;:&quot;replace&quot;,&quot;url&quot;:&quot;/content/canadasite/en/immigration-refugees-citizenship/test/enterprise/dli-list-revamp/designated-learning-institutions-list.html .ab&quot;,&quot;container&quot;:&quot;.prov&quot;}]">Alberta</li>
                                    <li data-wb-fieldflow="[{&quot;action&quot;: &quot;tblfilter&quot;,&quot;source&quot;: &quot;#dli-table-grad&quot;,&quot;column&quot;: 0,&quot;value&quot;: &quot;British Columbia&quot;},
                        {&quot;action&quot;:&quot;ajax&quot;, &quot;type&quot;:&quot;replace&quot;,&quot;url&quot;:&quot;/content/canadasite/en/immigration-refugees-citizenship/test/enterprise/dli-list-revamp/designated-learning-institutions-list.html .bc&quot;,&quot;container&quot;:&quot;.prov&quot;}]">British Columbia</li>
